// Macros for testing.

macro_rules! assert_ok {
    ($expr:expr, $value:expr) => (
        match $expr {
            Ok(v) => assert_eq!(v, $value),
            r => panic!("assertion failed: unexpected {:?}", r),
        }
    )
}

macro_rules! assert_pat {
    ($expr:expr, $pat:pat) => (
        match $expr {
            $pat => {},
            ref r => panic!("assertion failed: unexpected {:?}", r),
        }
    )
}

macro_rules! assert_err_pat {
    ($expr:expr, $variant:pat) => (
        match $expr {
            Err($variant) => {},
            r => panic!("assertion failed: unexpected {:?}", r),
        }
    )
}

// This macro is intended to be used with std::io::Error, but other
// types with kind() will also work.
macro_rules! assert_err_kind {
    ($expr:expr, $kind:expr) => (
        match $expr {
            Err(e) => assert_eq!(e.kind(), $kind),
            r => panic!("assertion failed: unexpected {:?}", r),
        }
    )
}

extern crate exif;

use std::env;
use std::fs::File;
use std::io::BufReader;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

fn main() {
    for path in env::args_os().skip(1).map(PathBuf::from) {
        if let Err(e) = dump_file(&path) {
            println!("{}: {}", path.display(), e);
        }
    }
}

fn dump_file(path: &Path) -> Result<(), exif::Error> {
    let file = File::open(path)?;

    // To parse strictly:
    // let exif = exif::Reader::new()
    //     .read_from_container(&mut BufReader::new(&file))?;

    // To parse with continue-on-error mode:
    let exif = exif::Reader::new()
        .continue_on_error(true)
        .read_from_container(&mut BufReader::new(&file))
        .or_else(|e| e.distill_partial_result(|errors| {
            eprintln!("{}: {} warning(s)", path.display(), errors.len());
            errors.iter().for_each(|e| eprintln!("  {}", e));
        }))?;

    println!("{}", path.display());
    for f in exif.fields() {
        println!("  {}/{}: {}",
                 f.ifd_num.index(), f.tag,
                 f.display_value().with_unit(&exif));
        println!("      {:?}", f.value);
    }
    Ok(())
}

use std::mem;

// This library assumes that usize is not smaller than u32.
#[test]
fn size_of_usize() {
    assert!(mem::size_of::<usize>() >= mem::size_of::<u32>());
}

/// The PUA code points special-cased in the GB18030 encoder.
pub(crate) static GB18030_2022_OVERRIDE_PUA: [u16; 18] = [
    0xE78D, 0xE78E, 0xE78F, 0xE790, 0xE791, 0xE792, 0xE793, 0xE794, 0xE795, 0xE796, 0xE81E, 0xE826,
    0xE82B, 0xE82C, 0xE832, 0xE843, 0xE854, 0xE864,
];

/// The bytes corresponding to the PUA code points special-cased in the GB18030 encoder.
pub(crate) static GB18030_2022_OVERRIDE_BYTES: [[u8; 2]; 18] = [
    [0xA6, 0xD9],
    [0xA6, 0xDA],
    [0xA6, 0xDB],
    [0xA6, 0xDC],
    [0xA6, 0xDD],
    [0xA6, 0xDE],
    [0xA6, 0xDF],
    [0xA6, 0xEC],
    [0xA6, 0xED],
    [0xA6, 0xF3],
    [0xFE, 0x59],
    [0xFE, 0x61],
    [0xFE, 0x66],
    [0xFE, 0x67],
    [0xFE, 0x6D],
    [0xFE, 0x7E],
    [0xFE, 0x90],
    [0xFE, 0xA0],
];

pub fn escape(text: &str) -> String {
    let mut quoted = String::new();
    escape_into(text, &mut quoted);
    quoted
}

fn escape_into(text: &str, buf: &mut String) {
    buf.reserve(text.len());
    for c in text.chars() {
        if is_meta_character(c) {
            buf.push('\\');
        }
        buf.push(c);
    }
}

fn is_meta_character(c: char) -> bool {
    match c {
        '\\' | '.' | '+' | '*' | '?' | '(' | ')' | '|' | '[' | ']' | '{' | '}' | '^' | '$'
        | '#' | '&' | '-' | '~' => true,
        _ => false,
    }
}

crates/ledger-import/fonts/NotoSans-LICENSE.txt
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fonts:
  crates/ledger-import/fonts/NotoSansArabicUI-Regular.ttf
  crates/ledger-import/fonts/NotoSansDevanagari-Regular.ttf
  crates/ledger-import/fonts/NotoSansHebrew-Regular.ttf
  crates/ledger-import/fonts/NotoSansThai-Regular.ttf

Noto Sans Arabic UI, Noto Sans Hebrew, Noto Sans Thai and Noto Sans
Devanagari, Regular (the PDFs' and the viewer's fallbacks for Arabic,
Hebrew, Thai and Devanagari text, which Inter lacks), unmodified:

  NotoSansArabicUI-Regular.ttf    Version 2.011   190,944 bytes
    Copyright 2022 The Noto Project Authors (https://github.com/notofonts/arabic)
    SHA-256 f4fe1866a64cba1e8b8d08a67cd29070f919edf778ca5de983c1f2a690fc953b
  NotoSansHebrew-Regular.ttf      Version 3.001    16,836 bytes
    Copyright 2024 The Noto Project Authors (https://github.com/notofonts/hebrew)
    SHA-256 04272f5600d0ec816d31d0df73b23aa8d3501ea359ebe820da31c11ffcf00853
  NotoSansThai-Regular.ttf        Version 2.002    20,960 bytes
    Copyright 2022 The Noto Project Authors (https://github.com/notofonts/thai)
    SHA-256 d4303fe9c63ebb72759ca8b6d2040c8ae81689f7d08d7b91c656154382b49313
  NotoSansDevanagari-Regular.ttf  Version 2.007   184,228 bytes
    Copyright 2022 The Noto Project Authors (https://github.com/notofonts/devanagari)
    SHA-256 9c7d935139ea6a1e6ad9dbac4f6d27ece1e04bca8123c8888d00a0f9df4724cd

Noto is a trademark of Google LLC. (These fonts name no Reserved Font
Name.)

Each is the static, unhinted Regular weight as the Noto project
publishes it (https://github.com/notofonts/notofonts.github.io), at the
last commit that changed it, retrieved 2026-10-20:

  fonts/NotoSansArabicUI/unhinted/ttf/NotoSansArabicUI-Regular.ttf
    commit 1ed0aaad710107ac0d6663ece26a4bb82ce6cff8
  fonts/NotoSansHebrew/unhinted/ttf/NotoSansHebrew-Regular.ttf
    commit 40ae54ff25c9e0ad997f62fde48297dd9dfe51c8
  fonts/NotoSansThai/unhinted/ttf/NotoSansThai-Regular.ttf
    commit 6da9d59fd18f429cb8dbfa87e87669204f77ddab
  fonts/NotoSansDevanagari/unhinted/ttf/NotoSansDevanagari-Regular.ttf
    commit 92d2ea744479d3379927f1fb5f8b0945dd0349b7

  https://raw.githubusercontent.com/notofonts/notofonts.github.io/<commit>/<path>

Arabic in its UI form, not Noto Sans Arabic itself (2.013): the same
design and project, but 2.013 draws a letter's dots as marks of their
own, which the PDFs' typesetter (Typst 0.15) writes as text runs of
their own, each carrying the letter again, so a PDF's text (copied,
searched, read aloud) would say "شش" for "ش" and "سلام" as "شلام"; the
UI form, whose letters keep their dots and whose height fits a Latin
line, reads back as written.

Unhinted, not hinted: the outlines are the same, the hinting tables
(about 180 KB across the four) serve only Windows' old rasterizer, and
neither the PDFs nor the viewer use them. Kept whole: Arabic and
Devanagari need their shaping tables to join and order their letters.

Emoji are not bundled: a colour emoji font is about 10 MB, so an emoji
in a name prints as an empty box in the PDFs (the viewer draws egui's
own small emoji set).

This Font Software is licensed under the SIL Open Font License,
Version 1.1.

This license is copied below, and is also available with a FAQ at:
https://openfontlicense.org

crates/ledger-import/fonts/NotoSansCJK-LICENSE.txt
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fonts:
  crates/ledger-import/fonts/NotoSansCJKsc-Regular-subset.otf

Noto Sans CJK SC Regular, version 2.004, subset:
NotoSansCJKsc-Regular-subset.otf (the PDFs' fallback for Chinese,
Japanese and Korean text, and the other characters below that Inter
lacks).

Copyright (c) 2014-2021 Adobe (http://www.adobe.com/).
Noto is a trademark of Google Inc.
(Noto Sans CJK names no Reserved Font Name.)

Made from Noto Sans CJK 2.004 (https://github.com/notofonts/noto-cjk),
NotoSansCJK-Regular.ttc as Arch Linux packages it (noto-fonts-cjk
20240730-1; SHA-256
b76b0433203017ca80401b2ee0dd69350349871c4b19d504c34dbdd80541690a),
face 2 (Noto Sans CJK SC), with fontTools 4.65.0, 2026-10-20:

  every code point at or above U+1100 that face maps and Inter Regular
  (Inter-Regular.ttf here) doesn't: 44,128 code points, among them all
  of Hangul, kana, bopomofo, CJK punctuation and fullwidth forms, and
  every CJK ideograph the face draws (20,976 of the basic block, 6,582
  of Extension A, the compatibility ideographs, and 2,310 from
  Extensions B to G), written as U+XXXX-YYYY ranges to cjk.txt, then

  pyftsubset NotoSansCJK-Regular.ttc --font-number=2 \
    --unicodes-file=cjk.txt --no-hinting --layout-features='' \
    --drop-tables+=BASE,VORG,vhea,vmtx,GPOS,GSUB \
    --output-file=NotoSansCJKsc-Regular-subset.otf

  SHA-256 of the result:
  ebf572d736e30b54bf5db087b15f323c16c465052aec788d9cc1d8db41884929

The subset is a Modified Version under the license below, which it
keeps.

This Font Software is licensed under the SIL Open Font License,
Version 1.1.

This license is copied below, and is also available with a FAQ at:
http://scripts.sil.org/OFL
